Binary symmetric local search details:
- n even, m=n/2. u ∈ {±1} length m, sum 0 (K=+1).
- Full z length n: z = tile? z[i]=u[i] for i<m, z[n-1-i]=u[i] for i<m.
- Objective C = max_b (N_b - R_b)/(2n) for b=0..n-1 (positive lags). R_b = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-b} z_i z_{i+b}. We can compute initial R vector via correlate (or custom). Max C.
- Maintaining R: For a swap of pair a (u[a]=+1) and pair b (u[b]=-1), swapping signs at full positions p=a, q=n-1-a, r=b, s=n-1-b. After swap, u[a] -1, u[b]+1. Full z changes: at p,q from + to - (delta -2); at r,s from - to + (delta +2).
For each lag b_lag, R_lag =∑ z_i z_{i+b} (positive index i). We can compute delta for all b_lag vector length n efficiently:
Delta = 0
For each changed position p with delta δ (old->new):
  contribution as first index: for lags e such that i+b in [0,n-1], term ∑ δ * z_final? Actually new R - old R = ∑ over pairs (i,i+e) (new_i new_j - old_i old_j), for e=0..n-1 where pairs valid.
If only one position changes, terms where i=p and j=p+e: δ * z_j (old or final? Since j unchanged, new=old; also old term old_i z_j, new δ+old_i times z_j => δ z_j). Terms where j=p and i=p-e: z_i δ. If e=0, term is diagonal and changes δ*? old_i^2 to new_i^2: for binary signs, old^2=new^2=1, delta 0. For non-binary, need δ(new_i-old_i)? But binary flip square unchanged.
If two changed positions interact, sequential formula using final z for neighbor if neighbor also changed? We can compute delta as sum over changed positions p of δ_p * (neighbor final? maybe). Let's derive: For product z_i z_j, new-old = (z_i+δ_i)(z_j+δ_j)-z_i z_j = δ_i z_j + z_i δ_j + δ_i δ_j. For a pair where both changed, include cross. If we sum δ_i z_j (old) over all pairs and also z_i δ_j, might double? Better to recompute contributions involving any changed position using old and new values. Since only 4 positions, for each e, the only pairs affected are those where i or i+e in P (changed set). Number O(|P|) per e. Can vectorize across e by precomputed neighbor arrays.
For each changed p, list indices i = p-e for e such that i in [0,p] (term i=p-e, j=p), and i=p with e such that p+e in [0,n) (term i=p, j=p+e). The total contribution new-old can be computed by for e vector:
old_contrib = z_old[i]*z_old[j] if valid; new_contrib = z_new[i]*z_new[j]. For e where both changed, included twice? If we sum over p, a pair with both p and q changed will be considered in p's first/second and q's first/second, potentially double count. Since each product term has one i and one j; it is unique e,i. We can avoid double by building affected pairs set: for each changed p, consider pairs where i=p (j=p+e) and i=p-e (j=p). For a pair both changed, it appears twice in this list (as first from p and second from q). Need unique by (i,e). But since P small, we can compute delta per e by explicit formula without double:
For each e, if no changed positions valid at i or i+e, delta=0. Otherwise delta = new_product_sum - old_product_sum over i in affected unique. We can compute in a loop over e? Vectorizing with unique is complex. But n small, we can compute delta array by a Python loop over 4 positions and avoid double by using old/new? Maybe double not too bad if we adjust.

But we can vectorize delta per swap with numpy arrays and include cross correction. Let's derive formula using final z to avoid double and binary squares.
For each changed p, with old value a_p, new value b_p, delta_p=b_p-a_p. For all e:
The total change in R_e = sum over all pairs (i,i+e) new_i new_j - old_i old_j.
We can express = sum_{p in P} [ δ_p (sum of neighbor values with other index old?) ] + sum_{p<q in P, |p-q|=e} Δ_p Δ_q? Let's use formula new-old = δ_i z_j^old + z_i^old δ_j + δ_i δ_j. Sum over all pairs. The first term sum over p δ_p * sum_j z_j^old where pair (p,j) with |p-j|=e. This includes both i=p and j=p cases. If pair both p,q, first term for p includes δ_p old_q, first term for q includes δ_q old_p, cross δpδq. The expression δ_i z_j + z_i δ_j + δ_iδ_j summed over pairs gives unique count: For pair p,q, if p as i, q as j: δ_p old_q + old_p δ_q + δpδq. If p as j, q as i (appropriate orientation), the pair is counted only once in sum over all pairs; our first sum over p over both i and j will include the pair twice? Let's be precise:
Term A = ∑_{p∈P} δ_p * ∑_{j: |j-p|=e, j in range} z_j^{old}. This includes for each incident edge (p,j) the change due to p holding neighbor old. For an edge with both endpoints changed, A includes δ_p old_q + δ_q old_p. Term B = ∑_{p<q∈P, |p-q|=e} δ_p δ_q adds cross. Then total for that edge = δ_p old_q + old_p δ_q + δpδq, which is exactly new-old. Good. It is not double counted because A has two contributions for two endpoints, which is intended.    S[:n-e,e] += z[e:]

def update_S(S, z, pos, dq):
    # z already updated? S should be updated by adding dq to rows p=e? For each row p and e=|p-pos|, S[p,e] += dq because S[row, e] includes z at pos if row±e=pos.
    if pos>0:
        p = np.arange(pos-1,-1,-1)
        e = pos - p  # 1..pos
        S[p,e] += dq
    if pos<n-1:
        p = np.arange(pos+1,n)
        e = p - pos
        S[p,e] += dq

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize? maybe not needed if using PG only; but maybe no scipy import? allowed but no need. We can skip minimize to save.

def compute_c5(h,n): ... dx=2/n; return max(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'))*dx
def project_feasible(h,n): target=n/2; h=clip; lam bisection; return clip
def sym_project(h,n): h=0.5*(h+h[::-1]); return project_feasible(h,n) maybe then sym again

def smooth_pg(h,n,iters,deadline,alpha=200):
 h=sym_project(h,n)
 for it in range(iters):
   if time.time()>deadline: break
   dx=2/n
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')*dx
   m=c.max()
   sh=alpha*(c-m)
   w=np.exp(sh); w/=w.sum()
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   # t=0
   grad += w[n-1]*(1 - 2*h)
   for r in range(1,n):
      wp=w[n-1+r]; wn=w[n-2-r]? Need index mapping: full c index 0..2n-2, t=0 at n-1, positive t=r at n-1+r, negative t=-r at n-1-r.
      wp=w[n-1+r]
      wn=w[n-1-r]
      if abs(wp)>1e-18 or abs(wn)>1e-18:
         a=n-r
         grad[:a] += wp*(1 - h[r:]) - wn*h[r:]
         grad[r:] += wn*(1 - h[:a]) - wp*h[:a]
   grad *= dx
   # sym gradient
   grad=0.5*(grad+grad[::-1])
   # line search
   base_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   gnorm=max(1e-12,np.max(np.abs(grad)))
   steps=[0.5/gnorm,0.25/gnorm,0.125/gnorm,0.0625/gnorm,0.03125/gnorm]
   improved=False
   for st in steps:
      h2=sym_project(h - st*grad,n)
      c2=compute_c5(h2,n)
      if c2 < base_c - 1e-12:
         h=h2; base_c=c2; improved=True; break
   if not improved:
      # maybe tiny random nudge?
      break
 return h, base_c
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-20; rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 # parent
 starts=[]
 try:
   hp=np.asarray(initial_h_values,float); n=int(len(hp))
 except: hp=np.full(400,0.5); n=400
 h=sym_project(hp,n); c=compute_c5(h,n); best=(h,c,n)
 # Add low-dim/fourier starts for best n and small n? create function make_starts(n, count)
 for start in make_starts(n): update
 # Binary search for nlist: small to parent n
 nlist = unique [80,120,160,240,320,400,500,640,700] filtered <= maybe 1000 and divisible4, time left.
 binary_h_best=[]
 for n_try in nlist:
   if time > deadline - 300: break
   h_bin,c_bin = binary_search(n_try, rng, deadline, time_slice)
   update best; store if c good
   # perhaps smooth_pg from h_bin
   if time left: h_p,c_p = smooth_pg(h_bin, n_try, 80, deadline-alpha); update
 # Polish best: take current best, try smooth_pg with noise

 for h,n in candidates sorted by c:
   if time...
       for amp in [0.02,0.05,0.1]:
          hp = sym_project(h + amp*rng.normal(0,1,n), n)
          hp2,c2 = smooth_pg(hp,n, 60, deadline)
          update
 # Multi-n interpolate best: choose n maybe len(best) +/-; skip if time
 # final project
 return best
```

 # min possible sum with shift l: if 1+? Actually clip(x+l) monotone.

def make_starts(n, rng, count=6):
    starts=[]
    x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
    # base starts dict? avoid closures
    # cos modes
    for m in range(1, min(9,n//2+1)):
       amp=1.0
       h=(1+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*n? /n?)) /2 use i? x[i] = 2*i/n -> cos(π m x)=cos(2π m i/n)
    # tent
    h=1-np.abs(x-1)
    # random even Fourier
    for r in range(count):
       maxmode=rng.randint(2,9)
       coeffs=rng.normal(0,0.6,maxmode)
       z=np.zeros(n)
       for m in range(1,maxmode+1): z += coeffs[m-1]*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*np.arange(n)/n)
       z=np.tanh(z)  # keeps [-1,1]
       h=(1+z)/2
    for each: h=sym_project(h,n); c=compute_c5; keep best? return list of maybe 4 lowest unique.
```

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    lo, hi = -2.0, 2.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s < target: lo = mid
        else: hi = mid
    h = np.clip(h + 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    # final tiny correction if needed: if sum still off, add uniform? but clip may.
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        # small correction on non-boundaries preserving sym? generic iterative 5
        for _ in range(100):
            if diff > 0:
                room = 1.0 - h; tot=room.sum()
            else:
                room = h; tot=h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15: break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff/tot)*room, 0, 1)
            diff = target - h.sum()
            if abs(diff) <= 1e-13: break
    return h
```
The bisection should be enough; final correction perhaps breaks symmetry if h not exactly? It uses room symmetric if h symmetric? For diff positive, room symmetric; symmetric. For diff negative, room h symmetric. Good.

`sym_project`:
```
def sym_project(h,n):
    h = 0.5*(h + h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
Since shift preserves symmetry? If h symmetric, clip(h+λ) symmetric; sum target. The final correction if any uses symmetric room, so symmetric. Good.

def make_starts(n, rng, count=8):

def binary_search(n, seed, time_limit):
    if n%4 !=0 or n<40 or time_limit<10: return (None,None)
    m=n//2; K=m//2
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    Nvec = n - np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    t_end = time.time()+time_limit
    best_c = 10.0; best_u=None; best_z=None
    styles=3
    for style in range(3):
       if style==0:
          u=np.full(m,-1.0); u[rng.choice(m,K,replace=False)]=1.0
       elif style==1:
          u=np.full(m,-1.0); u[(np.arange(K)+0.5)*(m//K).astype??] =1.0
```

def build_S(z):
 n=len(z); S=np.zeros((n,n));
 for e in range(1,n):
   S[:n-e,e] += z[e:]
   S[e:,e] += z[:n-e]
 return S
```

 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 cands=[]

    n0=400; h=np.full(n0,0.5); h=sym_project(h,n0); c=compute_c5(h,n0); cands.append((h,c,n0))
 if time.time() < deadline-60:
    for item in make_starts(n0, rng, 6):
       cands.append(item)
 # choose binary n list

 for nv in (120, 200, 300, 450, 600):
   if nv%4==0 and nv not in n_bio: n_bio.append(nv)

   # add nearby divisible by4 maybe min(1000,n0+?)
   for add in (0,1,2,-1,-2):
      nv=n0+add
      if nv%4==0 and nv>40 and nv<=1000 and nv not in n_bio: n_bio.append(nv); break
 # limit to 5? n_bio maybe 6.

 if time_left>150 and n_bio:
    total_bin=min(320.0, 0.35*time_left)
    per=max(12.0,total_bin/len(n_bio))
    for nv in n_bio:
       if time.time()>deadline-250: break
       ht,ct=binary_search(nv, seed+nv, per)
       if ht is not None:

 # polish top
 top=select_top(cands,8)
 for item in top:
   h0,c0,nv=item
   if time.time()>deadline-40: break
   if c0 > 0.9: continue
   if nv <= 200: its=80
   elif nv <= 400: its=60
   else: its=45
   for amp in (0.0,0.01,0.03,0.08,0.15):
      if time.time()>deadline-40: break
      if amp==0.0: hstart=h0.copy()
      else: hstart=h0 + amp*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,nv)

      h1,c1=smooth_pg(hstart,nv,its,deadline-20)
      if c1 < c0 - 1e-12:

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    lo, hi = -2.0, 2.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    for _ in range(80):
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            tot = (1.0 - h).sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * (1.0 - h), 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
        diff = target - h.sum()
    return h


def sym_project(h, n):
    h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_z_from_u(u):
    m = int(u.size)
    z = np.empty(2 * m, dtype=float)
    z[:m] = u
    z[m:] = u[::-1]
    return z


def build_S(z):
    n = int(z.size)
    S = np.zeros((n, n), dtype=float)
    for e in range(1, n):
        S[:n - e, e] += z[e:]
        S[e:, e] += z[:n - e]
    return S


def select_top(cands, k):
    if not cands:
        return []
    used = []
    top = []
    for _ in range(min(k, len(cands))):
        bi = -1
        bc = 1e100
        for i, item in enumerate(cands):
            if i in used:
                continue
            if item[1] < bc:
                bc = item[1]
                bi = i
        if bi < 0:
            break
        used.append(bi)
        top.append(cands[bi])
    return top


def make_starts(n, rng, count=6):
    out = []
    if n < 4:
        return out
    i = np.arange(n)
    x = 2.0 * i / float(n)
    base = []
    for m in range(1, min(5, n // 2 + 1)):
        base.append(0.5 * (1.0 + np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * i / float(n))))
    base.append(1.0 - np.abs(x - 1.0))
    base.append(np.abs(x - 1.0))
    for _ in range(max(0, int(count))):
        maxm = 2 + int(rng.randint(7))
        coeff = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, maxm)
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for m in range(1, maxm + 1):
            z += coeff[m - 1] * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * i / float(n))
        base.append(0.5 * (1.0 + np.tanh(z)))
    for arr in base:
        h = sym_project(np.asarray(arr, dtype=float), n)
        c = compute_c5(h, n)
        out.append((h, c, n))
    return out


def smooth_pg(h, n, iters, deadline):
    h = sym_project(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    stages = ((50.0, 0.30), (200.0, 0.40), (800.0, 0.30))
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    for alpha, frac in stages:
        stage_iters = max(1, int(iters * frac))
        fails = 0
        for _ in range(stage_iters):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
                if best_c < compute_c5(best_h, n):
                    best_h = h.copy()
                return best_h, best_c
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx
            m = float(c.max())
            sh = alpha * (c - m)
            sh = np.maximum(sh, -50.0)
            w = np.exp(sh)
            ws = w.sum()
            if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                w = np.zeros_like(w)
                w[n - 1] = 1.0
            else:
                w /= ws
            grad = w[n - 1] * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
            for r in range(1, n):
                wp = w[n - 1 + r]
                wn = w[n - 1 - r]
                if abs(wp) < 1e-18 and abs(wn) < 1e-18:
                    continue
                a = n - r
                grad[:a] += wp * (1.0 - h[r:]) - wn * h[r:]
                grad[r:] += wn * (1.0 - h[:a]) - wp * h[:a]
            grad *= dx
            grad = 0.5 * (grad + grad[::-1])
            gnorm = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
            if gnorm < 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(gnorm):
                fails += 4
                break
            rates = (0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125, 0.015625, 0.0078125)
            improved = False
            improved_c = m
            for rate in rates:
                st = min(3.0, rate / gnorm)
                h2 = sym_project(h - st * grad, n)
                c2 = compute_c5(h2, n)
                if c2 < m - 1e-12:
                    h = h2
                    improved = True
                    improved_c = c2
                    break
            if improved:
                fails = 0
                if improved_c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    best_c = improved_c
            else:
                fails += 1
                if fails > 6:
                    break
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    if best_c < compute_c5(best_h, n):
        best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def binary_search(n, seed, time_limit):
    if n % 4 != 0 or n < 40 or time_limit < 10.0:
        return None, None
    m = n // 2
    K = m // 2
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    t_end = time.time() + float(time_limit)
    Nvec = float(n) - np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    cur_c = 1e100
    cur_u = cur_z = cur_R = cur_S = None
    sign_vec = np.array([-2.0, -2.0, 2.0, 2.0], dtype=float)
    for style in range(3):
        if style == 0:
            u = np.full(m, -1.0, dtype=float)
            u[rng.choice(m, K, replace=False)] = 1.0
        elif style == 1:
            u = np.full(m, -1.0, dtype=float)
            u[0:m:2] = 1.0
        else:
            u = np.full(m, 1.0, dtype=float)
            u[1:m:2] = -1.0
        z = make_z_from_u(u)
        R = np.correlate(z, z, mode="full")[n - 1:].astype(float)
        S = build_S(z)
        cvec = (Nvec - R) / (2.0 * float(n))
        cc = float(cvec.max())
        if cc < cur_c:
            cur_c = cc
            cur_u = u.copy()
            cur_z = z.copy()
            cur_R = R.copy()
            cur_S = S.copy()
    if cur_u is None:
        return None, None
    u = cur_u
    z = cur_z
    R = cur_R
    S = cur_S
    ccur = cur_c
    best_c = cur_c
    best_z = z.copy()
    B = 96 if n <= 250 else (64 if n <= 500 else 40)
    rows_all = None
    no_imp = 0
    accept_count = 0
    while time.time() < t_end and no_imp < 6000:
        plus_idx = np.flatnonzero(u > 0.0)
        minus_idx = np.flatnonzero(u < 0.0)
        if plus_idx.size == 0 or minus_idx.size == 0:
            break
        a = plus_idx[rng.randint(0, plus_idx.size, size=B)]
        b = minus_idx[rng.randint(0, minus_idx.size, size=B)]
        P = np.empty((B, 4), dtype=int)
        P[:, 0] = a
        P[:, 1] = n - 1 - a
        P[:, 2] = b
        P[:, 3] = n - 1 - b
        SPA = np.take(S, P, axis=0)
        delta = (SPA * sign_vec[None, :, None]).sum(axis=1)
        d01 = np.abs(P[:, 0] - P[:, 1])
        d02 = np.abs(P[:, 0] - P[:, 2])
        d03 = np.abs(P[:, 0] - P[:, 3])
        d12 = np.abs(P[:, 1] - P[:, 2])
        d13 = np.abs(P[:, 1] - P[:, 3])
        d23 = np.abs(P[:, 2] - P[:, 3])
        if rows_all is None or rows_all.size != B:
            rows_all = np.arange(B)
        for dvec, val in ((d01, 4.0), (d02, -4.0), (d03, -4.0),
                          (d12, -4.0), (d13, -4.0), (d23, 4.0)):
            mask = dvec > 0
            if np.any(mask):
                delta[rows_all[mask], dvec[mask]] += val
        c_new = (Nvec[None, :] - R[None, :] - delta).max(axis=1) / (2.0 * float(n))
        i = int(np.argmin(c_new))
        cni = float(c_new[i])
        accepted = False
        if cni < ccur - 1e-12:
            accepted = True
        elif no_imp > 250 and rng.rand() < 0.08 and cni < ccur + 0.007:
            accepted = True
        if accepted:
            pos = P[i]
            oldz = z[pos].copy()
            newz = np.array([-1.0, -1.0, 1.0, 1.0], dtype=float)
            dq = newz - oldz
            z[pos] = newz
            for pp in range(4):
                ppos = int(pos[pp])
                dval = float(dq[pp])
                if dval == 0.0:
                    continue
                if ppos > 0:
                    rr = np.arange(ppos - 1, -1, -1)
                    ee = ppos - rr
                    S[rr, ee] += dval
                if ppos < n - 1:
                    rr = np.arange(ppos + 1, n)
                    ee = rr - ppos
                    S[rr, ee] += dval
            u[int(pos[0])] = -1.0
            u[int(pos[2])] = 1.0
            R = R + delta[i]
            ccur = cni
            accept_count += 1
            no_imp = 0
            if cni < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = cni
                best_z = z.copy()
                best_u = u.copy()
        else:
            no_imp += 1
    if best_z is None:
        best_z = z
    h = 0.5 * (1.0 + best_z)
    h = sym_project(h, n)
    c = compute_c5(h, n)
    return h, c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 25.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    cands = []

    parent = globals().get("initial_h_values", None)
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            h0 = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
            n0 = int(h0.size)
            if n0 > 4:
                h0 = sym_project(h0, n0)
                c0 = compute_c5(h0, n0)
                cands.append((h0, c0, n0))
        except Exception:
            n0 = 400
    if len(cands) == 0:
        n0 = 400
        h0 = sym_project(np.full(n0, 0.5, dtype=float), n0)
        c0 = compute_c5(h0, n0)
        cands.append((h0, c0, n0))

    if time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
        cands.extend(make_starts(n0, rng, 6))

    n_bio = []
    for nv in (120, 200, 300, 400, 500, 640):
        if nv % 4 == 0 and nv < 1000 and nv not in n_bio:
            n_bio.append(nv)
    if n0 % 4 == 0 and n0 < 1000 and n0 not in n_bio:
        n_bio.append(n0)
    elif n0 % 4 != 0:
        for delta_n in (1, -1, 2, -2):
            nv = n0 + delta_n
            if 40 <= nv <= 1000 and nv % 4 == 0 and nv not in n_bio:
                n_bio.append(nv)
                break

    time_left = deadline - time.time()
    if time_left > 140.0 and len(n_bio) > 0:
        total_bin = min(320.0, 0.36 * time_left)
        per_bin = max(12.0, total_bin / float(len(n_bio)))
        for nv in n_bio:
            if time.time() > deadline - 260.0:
                break
            ht, ct = binary_search(nv, int(seed) + int(nv), per_bin)
            if ht is not None:
                ht = sym_project(ht, nv)
                ct = compute_c5(ht, nv)
                cands.append((ht, ct, nv))

    top = select_top(cands, 8)
    for item in top:
        h0, c0, nv = item
        if time.time() > deadline - 50.0:
            break
        if not np.isfinite(c0) or c0 > 0.9:
            continue
        if nv <= 200:
            its = 80
        elif nv <= 450:
            its = 60
        else:
            its = 45
        for amp in (0.0, 0.01, 0.03, 0.08, 0.15):
            if time.time() > deadline - 50.0:
                break
            if amp == 0.0:
                hstart = sym_project(h0, nv)
            else:
                hstart = h0 + amp * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, nv)
                hstart = sym_project(hstart, nv)
            h1, c1 = smooth_pg(hstart, nv, its, deadline - 25.0)
            if np.isfinite(c1) and c1 < c0 - 1e-12:
                cands.append((h1, c1, nv))
                break

    if len(cands) > 0 and time.time() < deadline - 25.0:
        top1 = select_top(cands, 1)
        h0, c0, nv = top1[0]
        its = 30 if nv <= 350 else 20
        h1, c1 = smooth_pg(h0, nv, its, deadline - 5.0)
        if np.isfinite(c1) and c1 < c0 - 1e-12:
            cands.append((h1, c1, nv))

    if not cands:
        n0 = 400
        h0 = sym_project(np.full(n0, 0.5, dtype=float), n0)
        c0 = compute_c5(h0, n0)
        return h0, c0, n0

    finale = select_top(cands, 1)[0]
    h, c, n = finale
    h = sym_project(h, n)
    c = compute_c5(h, n)
    if not np.isfinite(c):
        h = sym_project(np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float), n)
        c = compute_c5(h, n)
    return h, c, n
```
